Kết quả chấm bài

Substantiated behaviors: Sample_svc.exe copy itself to C:\ProgramData\TimeSync\tsyncsvc.exe (E02), service writes heartbeat logs (E04), no network activity (E05). Defects: Missing hash (E01), missing timeline coherence, missing registry persistence details, missing network activity evidence, placeholder timestamps, unresolved evidence references.
